Optimizer A: Natural gradient with active weights and adaptive step.
Pseudo:
```
def optimize_natural(a, time_budget):
    p = normalize a
    best=p, best_score=score(p)
    tau = initial (e.g., 0.2 / n? actually c scale 1/n; use tau = 0.1/n)
    lr = 1.0
    while time:
       c=conv(p,p); M=max
       # choose weights: soft with tau, or top active
       if use_top:
          threshold = M - 0.05*(M - min?) or M*0.99
          w = (c>=threshold).astype(float); w/=w.sum()
          # maybe weight by excess over threshold
       else:
          w=softmax((c-M)/tau)
       grad=2*corr(w,p)
       # normalize gradient to have max 1
       g = grad - p @ grad? maybe for natural? For exponential update, normalization factor accounts for sum; but using p*exp(-lr*grad), normalization removes constant component. So we can use centered gradient to reduce scale: grad_c = grad - np.dot(p, grad) (since exp(-lr grad), normalization effect = -lr (dot) constant, irrelevant. Use centered to improve conditioning.
       # choose lr: start maybe 1.0; try p_new = p*exp(-lr*grad_c); p_new/=sum
       # line search on exact score: for step factors [lr, lr*0.5,...] accept if score improves or J improves.
       if no improve, reduce lr, maybe adjust tau.
```
Need c computation fast. For n=3000, direct np.convolve maybe okay. But iterative many steps. Use FFT:
```
def convolve_pair(a):
    n=len(a); N=1
    while N<2*n-1: N*=2
    fa=fft(a, N); c=ifft(fa*fa).real[:2*n-1]
```
Since a real nonnegative, self-convolution real. Use scipy.fft for speed? We can import scipy.fft. Or numpy.fft. Use next power of 2. For N=8192 for n=3000. Very fast ~0.005s? In Python maybe 0.001. Good.
Gradient d=∑p_l w_{i+l}. Use FFT as derived:
```
r = w[::-1]
conv = ifft(fft(p,N2)*fft(r,N2)).real
d = conv[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
```
Need N2 length n + (2n-1)-1 =3n-2? Wait convolve p length n and r length 2n-1 => length 3n-2. Need FFT next pow2 >=3n-2. For n=3000, N2=16384 (if 3n=9000; pow2 16384). If using scipy.fft concrete? Could use numpy. This is 2 FFTs per gradient (p and r) plus one ifft. But r changes every step; can't reuse. Could compute gradient via direct np.correlate for n=3000 maybe 9M ops C ~0.01s; FFT 16k three transforms ~0.01s. Similar. For n=10000, FFT much better.
But we can combine c and grad: c uses p FFT length 2n. Grad uses p and r. We already have P_fft from c if same N? N_c=nextpow2(2n-1). N_g=nextpow2(3n-2), different. Let's design mirror with backtracking exact score:
```
def mirror_opt(p, time_budget, verbose):
    n=len(p); p=p/sum(p); best=p.copy(); best_score=score(p)
    t0=time.monotonic(); lr=1.0; tau=0.2/n; it=0; no_improve=0
    while time<t0+time_budget:
       c=conv(p,p); M=max(c)
       # weights: use soft with tau relative to M*0.05? 
       # To focus max, tau = max(1e-8, 0.05/M) ? c scale 1/n. If M~1.5/n=5e-5, tau=0.05*M? We can use tau = 0.05 * (M - percentile?)
       # A stable surrogate: weights = softmax((c - M)/gap), gap = max(1e-3*M, (M-min_pos)?). 
       gap = np.percentile(c[c>0], 95) - M? negative.
```

Implement L-BFGS helper:
```
def soft_obj_grad_b(b, tau):
    p = np.exp(b - b.max()); p /= p.sum()
    c = conv(p,p)
    z = c / tau
    z -= z.max()
    e = np.exp(z); q = e/e.sum()
    # J = 2n*tau*(z.max()+log(e.sum()))? But constant n; for optimization use taulogsumexp.
    val = 2*n*tau*(c.max() + np.log(e.sum()))  # since shifted cmax
    grad_p = 2*corr(q,p)
    # grad_b = p*(grad_p - sum p*grad_p)
    return val, p*(grad_p - np.dot(p,grad_p))
```

Let's design FFT functions.
```
import numpy as np
from scipy.fft import fft, ifft
# maybe use scipy.fft.next_pow2? Implement.
def next_pow2(m): return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()

def conv_sq_fft(a):
    n=a.size
    L=2*n-1
    N=1 << (L-1).bit_length()
    fa=fft(a, N)
    c=ifft(fa*fa).real[:L]
    # remove tiny imaginary
    return c
```
For self-convolution of real, using `fa * fa` (not conjugate) because polynomial square, not power spectrum. Correct: convolution a*a -> Fourier transform product A(k)*A(k), not |A|^2. So no conjugate. Since ifft may have negative tiny? Values nonnegative mathematically, but due to roundoff tiny negative. We can clip? For objective max, tiny negative irrelevant. But for softmax, tiny negative okay. Could `c[c<0 & c>-1e-12]=0`? Not necessary.

Gradient d for weights w length L and p:
```
def grad_from_weights(w,p):
    n=p.size; L=2*n-1
    N=1 << (3*n-3 -1).bit_length()? length conv = n + L -1 =3n-2
    N=1 << (3*n-2 -1).bit_length()
    fp=fft(p,N)
    fw=fft(w[::-1],N)
    conv=ifft(fp*fw).real
    return 2.0 * conv[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
```
Check length: conv length N, valid first 3n-2. Slice n-1 to 2n-1 inclusive length n. For n=1, edge? n>1. Test conceptually. Could use `np.correlate(w,p,'valid')` for exact and slower; maybe use direct if n small. We can implement both and verify occasionally? In final code cannot run. Use FFT for n>2000, direct for small to avoid bugs? But we need gradient for n=8192. Let's double-check FFT gradient with derivation and numpy correlate convention.

Numpy correlate(a, v, mode='valid') for a length M, v N (M>=N): y[k]=∑_{n=0}^{N-1} a[k+n] * v[n]. If a=w, v=p, y_i=∑_{l=0}^{n-1} w[i+l] p[l]. Our d_i = that. Good.
FFT convolution: p * r where r[j]=w[L-1-j]. conv[m]=∑_{l=0}^{n-1} p[l] r[m-l]=∑ p[l] w[L-1-m+l]. We want ∑ p[l] w[i+l]. Set L-1-m+l = i+l => m=L-1-i. Yes. For i=0, m=L-1=2n-2. i=n-1,m=n-1. So d=conv[2n-2:n-1:-1] equivalent slice n-1:2n-1 reversed. Great.
Need note convolution linear order: np.convolve(p, r) = p * r, commutative. FFT product fp*fr. Good.
Potential issue: `fft` default expects complex; `ifft` output length N; slice. Good.

```
def exact_score(p):
    c=conv_sq_fft(p)
    return 2*p.size*max(c)
```

Soft objective: c=conv(p,p). We want minimize objective = 2n*tau*logsumexp(c/tau). Since c values small. Compute:
```
z = c / tau
zmax = z.max()
e = np.exp(z - zmax)
s = e.sum()
val = 2n*tau*(zmax + np.log(s))
w = e/s
grad_p = grad_from_weights(w,p)
mean = np.dot(p, grad_p)
grad_b = p * (grad_p - mean)
```

Let's implement `run_mirror` well. It can run until time budget and use exact score.

Mirror algorithm details:
```
def run_mirror(p, time_budget, tau_factor=0.1, verbose=False):
    n=len(p); p=np.maximum(p,0); p/=p.sum()
    best=p.copy(); best_score=exact_score(p)
    t0=time.monotonic(); lr=1.0; it=0; fail=0
    tau = tau_factor / n  # initial
    # maybe maintain tau decreasing slowly
    while time.remaining>1:
       progress = elapsed/budget
       tau = (tau_factor / n) * (1.0 - 0.7*progress)  # down to 0.3 tau_factor/n
       c=conv_sq_fft(p); M=c.max()
       # if M <= best_score/(2n) maybe update best
       if 2*n*M < best_score*(1-1e-12): best...
       # weights: soft with tau, but if tau very small, use top active? We can use w=exp((c-M)/tau)
       z=(c-M)/tau
       z[z>0? all <=0] -> exp; sum. To avoid underflow, shift M.
       w=np.exp(z); w_sum=w.sum(); w/=w_sum
       grad=grad_from_weights(w,p)
       # center gradient to enforce sum invariance for natural update
       g=grad - np.dot(p, grad)
       gmax=np.abs(g).max()
       if gmax==0: break
       # target step: try eta = lr * 0.2 / gmax? We want lr*gmax ~0.2
       eta = lr * 0.2 / gmax
       # Multiplicative update
       # To avoid underflow, clip exponent
       e = np.clip(-eta*g, -50, 50)
       p_new = p * np.exp(e); p_new /= p_new.sum()
       score_new = exact_score(p_new) # 2n max
       # accept if better than current score or soft J? We'll accept if score_new < current_score*(1-1e-9) or < best_score
       # current_score = 2n*M
       if score_new < 2*n*M * (1 - 1e-12) or score_new < best_score*(1-1e-9):
           p=p_new; lr = min(lr*1.2, 20.0)
       else:
           # line search reduce eta by 0.5 up to 5
           accepted=False
           for _ in range(5):
              eta *=0.5
              ... if score_new < current*(1-1e-12) or <best? accept
              if accept: lr = min(lr*1.1,20); break
           if not accepted:
              lr = max(lr*0.5, 0.05)
              fail +=1
              if fail>20:
                  # perturb best and reset
                  p = best.copy(); add small random; p/=p.sum(); fail=0; lr=1
       it +=1
    return best
```
Computing exact_score(p_new) does FFT conv. Current c already computed; current_score=2n*M. For line search multiple, more convs. Could be okay. We can use soft objective for line search to avoid exact max? Exact is just max of c; we still need c_new. Same cost.

But mirror update may be too slow and conservative. We can enhance by using Euclidean projected gradient for larger steps. Let's implement `run_projected_grad` with correct soft gradient and line search. It can handle zeros and use derived step.

Projected gradient onto simplex:
Given p (sum1, nonneg). g = gradient of objective (soft or active). Choose step α. p_new = project(p - α g). Need gradient scale. For soft J (val n*J), gradient g_p = n*grad_p maybe. Step size from Lipschitz? We can backtracking.

But exact objective max non-smooth; use soft. Projected gradient with small τ can make sharp moves. Use adaptive α:
- Estimate L = 2 * max(i) *? For self-conv, Hessian can be large due p_i^2 terms. But backtracking.

Pseudo:
```
def project_simplex(a, S=1): ... (sort or bisection)
def run_pg(p, time_budget):
  tau = 0.05/n; lr=0.1
  while:
    c=conv; M; w=softmax((c-M)/tau)
    g=grad_from_weights(w,p) # for J
    # scale g to max1 then lr absolute maybe target move 0.1
    gmax=np.abs(g).max(); step=lr/gmax? 
    for factors: 
       p_new=project(p - factor*g)
       if score_new < current: accept
    ...
```

Projection onto simplex for sum1 with nonneg:
```
def project_simplex(v, S=1.0):
    n=v.size
    # sort method O(n log n)
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.nonzero(u*(np.arange(1,n+1))*1.0 > (css-1.0))[0][-1] # for S=1? General: u*(idx) > (css-S)/? Let's derive.
```
Standard: project v onto {x>=0, sum=S}. Find τ such that max(v-τ,0) sum=S. Sort descending u. For k candidates, τ = (sum(v[0:k]) - S)/k. Need largest k s.t. u[k] > τ. Using cumulative: ρ = max {j: u_j - (css_j - S)/j > 0}. 1-index j. Implement:
```
css=np.cumsum(u)
idx=np.arange(1,n+1, dtype=float)
cond = (u - (css - S)/idx) > 0
rho = np.where(cond)[0][-1] + 1
tau = (css[rho-1] - S)/rho
return np.maximum(v - tau, 0)
```
This is O(n log n). Good.

Soft objective and gradient wrt θ:
```
def param_soft_val_grad(theta, base, phi, tau):
    p = softmax(base + phi @ theta)
    c=conv_sq_fft(p); z... val=n*tau*(...); w
    gp=grad_from_weights(w,p)
    # softmax gradient: dp_i/dtheta_m = p_i*(phi_i_m - sum_i p_i phi_i_m)
    # grad_theta_m = sum_i p_i*(phi_i_m - mean_phi_m)*gp_i
    mean_phi = phi.T @ p  # length M
    s = p*gp
    grad_theta = phi.T @ s - mean_phi @ s
    return val, grad_theta
```
This needs `phi.T @ s` O(2nM). For n=8192,M=100 =>1.6M per eval, okay. Could be comparable to FFT.

Let's write `softmax_b(b)`:
```
def softmax_b(b):
    bm = b - np.max(b)
    p = np.exp(bm)
    s = p.sum()
    if not finite or s<=0: p = np.full(len(b),1/len(b))
    else: p /= s
    return p
```
In L-BFGS, if b has inf/nan, return inf.

Soft val grad b:
```
def soft_val_grad_b(b, tau):
    n=len(b)
    if n<2 or tau<=0: return inf, zeros
    p=softmax_b(b)
    c=conv_sq_fft(p)
    cmax=c.max()
    z=(c-cmax)/tau
    e=np.exp(z)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: val=float('inf'); grad=np.zeros(n)
    else:
       w=e/s
       val=n*tau*(cmax/tau + np.log(s))? Wait cmax/tau + log s. Since zmax=0. logsumexp(c/tau)=cmax/tau + log s. val = n*tau*(cmax/tau + log s) = n*(cmax + tau log s). Simpler avoid division by tau? But if tau small cmax/tau large times tau okay. Use `val = n * tau * (cmax / tau + np.log(s))`. If tau tiny, cmax/tau maybe 1e6, times tau 1e-? okay but float maybe. Use `val = n*(cmax + tau*np.log(s))`? Since cmax + tau log s. Check: τ*(cmax/τ + log s)=cmax + τ log s. Yes. But constant n*cmax may be large? cmax~1e-4*n=1? okay. However the additive cmax is always positive; log s negative. Optimizing cmax+τ log s. Good. Use `val = n*(cmax + tau*np.log(s))`.
       gp=grad_from_weights(w,p)
       gm=np.dot(p, gp)
       grad = n*p*(gp-gm)
    return val, grad
```

But `val = n*(cmax + tau log s)`: as τ→0, val→n*cmax; score =2n*cmax, so val=score/2. Good.

`mirror_opt` details:
```
def mirror_opt(p, time_budget, seed=0, rescue=None):
    n=p.size; p=np.maximum(p,0); p/=p.sum()
    best=p.copy(); best_score=score_of(p)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0=time.monotonic(); end=t0+time_budget
    lr=1.0; fail=0; it=0
    tau_factor=0.1
    # maybe tau in absolute = tau_factor * current M, annealed
    while time.monotonic() < end:
       c=conv_sq_fft(p); M=c.max(); cur_score=2*n*M
       if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12: best=p.copy(); best_score=cur_score
       if M<=0: break
       progress=min(1.0,(time.monotonic()-t0)/max(time_budget,1e-6))
       tau= (tau_factor * (1.0-0.6*progress)) * M
       if tau < 1e-12*M: tau=1e-12*M
       w=softmax_weights(c, tau)
       g=grad_from_weights(w,p)
       g -= np.dot(p,g)
       gmax=np.abs(g).max()
       if gmax < 1e-15: break
       step=0.25 * lr / gmax  # max relative change e^0.25
       accepted=False
       # To save curve, maybe first try without full line search? We'll do backtracking up to 3
       for attempt in range(4):
           e=np.exp(np.clip(-step*g, -30, 30))
           p_new = p*e; s=p_new.sum(); if s<=0: break
           p_new /= s
           sc_new = score_of(p_new)
           if sc_new < cur_score*(1.0 - 1e-12) or sc_new < best_score*(1.0 - 1e-12):
               p=p_new; accepted=True
               if sc_new < best_score: best_score=sc_new; best=p_new.copy()
               lr=min(lr*1.15, 10.0)
               break
           step *=0.5
       if not accepted:
           lr=max(lr*0.6, 0.05)
           fail+=1
           if fail>30:
              # reset to best with noise
              p=best.copy()
              if rescue is not None and rescue.size==n:
                  mix = float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.2))
                  p = (1-mix)*p + mix*rescue
              p = np.maximum(p,0)
              p = p * np.exp(0.1*rng.standard_normal(n))
              p = np.maximum(p,0); s=p.sum(); p=p/s if s>0 else best.copy()
              lr=1.0; fail=0
       it+=1
    return best, best_score
```
This will evaluate `score_of(p_new)` each attempt (FFT). If accepted, one FFT; if fail 4 FFTs. Could be okay. But many iterations maybe. We can use current c for current, and if step small maybe score_new approximate? No.

Maybe we can reduce FFT cost by using soft objective for acceptance with same weights? For a new p, need c_new; same cost. Exact max just max. Fine.

Project gradient could make larger steps but line search similar.

1. Utilities:
```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.fft import fft, ifft
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import warnings

def _next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
```
Need no underscores? Top-level fine.

`conv_sq_fft(a)`:
```
def conv_sq_fft(a):
    n = a.size
    L = 2*n - 1
    N = _next_pow2(L)
    fa = fft(a, N)
    c = ifft(fa * fa).real[:L]
    # clip small negative? maybe c[c < 0 and c > -1e-15] = 0
    return c
```
If a is integer? cast float in caller.

`score_of(p)`:
```
def score_of(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    s=p.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s): return float('inf')
    p = np.maximum(p,0.0) + 0.0
    # if clipped changed sum, renorm? For score scale invariant, if original p negative clipped, not same. We only pass nonnegative.
    p = p / s  # if s after clip? If clipped, s wrong; do clip before sum.
```
Better:
```
def normalize_nonneg(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,float).ravel(); p=np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0); p=np.maximum(p,0); s=p.sum(); if s<=0... return None; return p/s
```
Then score uses normalized p:
```
def score_of(p):
    pn=normalize_nonneg(p)
    if pn is None: return inf
    c=conv_sq_fft(pn)
    return float(2*pn.size*max(0.0, float(c.max())))
```
This normalizes each call; extra clip but fine. For optimization, p already normalized; we can have `score_normalized(p)` to avoid copy. But accuracy okay. To reduce overhead, use `score_norm(p)` assuming normalized:
```
def score_norm(p):
    n=p.size; c=conv_sq_fft(p); return 2*n*max(0.0, float(c.max()))
```
Use in loops. For pool add, normalize then score_norm.

`normalize_nonneg` top-level.

`grad_from_weights(w,p)`:
```
def grad_from_weights(w, p):
    n=p.size; L=2*n-1
    N=_next_pow2(3*n-2)
    fp=fft(p,N)
    fw=fft(w[::-1],N)
    conv=ifft(fp*fw).real
    d=conv[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
    return 2.0*d
```
For n=1, N=_next_pow2(1)=1? 3n-2=1. L=1, conv slice n-1:2n-1 =0:1 reversal length1. d=conv[0]. c0=p0^2. grad? w0=1, derivative of c0 wrt p0=2p0. d=p0? formula p0*w0=p0 =>2p0 good.

`weights_from_c(c, tau)`:
```
def weights_from_c(c, tau):
    M=float(c.max())
    if M <=0: w=zeros; w[0]=1; return w
    if not np.isfinite(tau) or tau <= 1e-300:
       w=np.zeros_like(c); idx=np.argmax(c); # maybe uniform over exact max?
       maxset = c >= M - 1e-15*M
       w[maxset]=1; w/=w.sum(); return w
    z=(c - M)/tau
    e=np.exp(z) # z<=0
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite:
       w=zeros; w[argmax]=1
    else: w=e/s
    return w
```
If tau relative to M, okay.

`project_simplex(v, S=1.0)` as above. Ensure v finite.

`softmax_prob_from_log(x)`:
```
def softmax_prob(x):
    x=np.asarray(x,float)
    if not finite all: return None
    x=x - np.max(x)
    p=np.exp(x)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=0: return None
    p/=s
    return p
```

`log_prob(p)`:
```
def log_prob(p):
    p=np.maximum(p,1e-300)
    b=np.log(p)
    b -= b.mean()
    return b
```

`soft_val_grad_b(b, tau)`:
```
def soft_val_grad_b(b, tau):
    n=len(b)
    if n<2: return inf, np.zeros(n)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(b)): return inf, zeros
    p=softmax_prob(b)
    if p is None: return inf, zeros
    c=conv_sq_fft(p)
    M=float(c.max())
    if not finite M: return inf, zeros
    tau=max(float(tau), 1e-300)
    z=(c-M)/tau
    e=np.exp(z); s=e.sum()
    if s<=0: w=zeros...
    else: w=e/s
    val = n*(M + tau*np.log(s))
    gp = grad_from_weights(w,p)
    gm = float(np.dot(p,gp))
    grad = n * p * (gp - gm)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)): grad=zeros
    return float(val), grad
```
Potential: c has negative tiny, M positive. `z` for very negative c (like -1e-12) / tau maybe -1000, underflow to 0; fine. `s` at least exp(0)=1 (for max), so positive.

Parameter val grad:
```
def param_soft_val_grad(theta, base_log, phi, tau):
    n=base_log.size
    if phi.shape[1]==0: p=softmax(base_log); ...
    lin=base_log + phi @ theta
    p=softmax_prob(lin)
    if p is None: return inf, zeros
    c=conv_sq_fft(p); M=...
    z... w...
    val=n*(M+tau*np.log(s))
    gp=grad_from_weights(w,p)
    svec=p*gp
    total_s=float(np.sum(svec))
    # grad theta
    if phi.size==0: grad=zeros
    else:
       mean_phi=phi.T @ p  # length M
       grad=phi.T @ svec - mean_phi * total_s
       # multiply by n? gp already? We need val gradient includes n. In soft_val we included n in grad by gp? Wait grad_from_weights returns 2d for J. For val=n*J, grad_p = n*grad_from_weights. In param above, if we use gp=grad_from_weights, need multiply by n. So svec=n*p*gp or grad = n*(...). Let's set gp = n * grad_from_weights(w,p).
    return val, grad
```
Define `gp = n * grad_from_weights(w,p)`. Then grad_theta formula. Good.

Similarly in `soft_val_grad_b`, `gp=grad_from_weights`; `grad = n*p*(gp-gm)`. Good.

`make_basis(n, seed)`:
```
def make_basis(n, seed):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    cols=[]
    # low integer Fourier: frequencies 1..20
    max_nf = min(20, max(4, n//16))
    for q in range(1, max_nf+1):
        cols.append(np.cos(2*np.pi*q*x))
        cols.append(np.sin(2*np.pi*q*x))
    # chirp: q=1..10
    max_ch = min(10, max(2, n//128))
    for q in range(1, max_ch+1):
        cols.append(np.cos(np.pi*q*x*x))
        cols.append(np.sin(np.pi*q*x*x))
    # random sinusoids: choose 10 freqs (integer or float)
    n_rand = max(4, min(20, 128 - len(cols)))
    for _ in range(n_rand):
       # frequency integer random 1..n//4
       q = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n//4)))
       # maybe random phase but phase can be absorbed by cos/sin; use both
       ph = float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
       cols.append(np.cos(2*np.pi*q*x + ph))
       cols.append(np.sin(2*np.pi*q*x + ph))
    phi=np.column_stack(cols)
    # remove zero variance and center
    for j in range(phi.shape[1]):
       std=phi[:,j].std()
       if std < 1e-12: phi[:,j]=0
       else: phi[:,j] = (phi[:,j]-phi[:,j].mean())/std
    return phi
```
Total cols maybe 40+20+40=100. Good. For small n, adjust. Centering removes constant per column; base handles bias.

```
def run_param_opt(base_p, time_budget, seed=0):
   n=base_p.size
   if n<50 or time_budget<30: return base_p, score_norm(base_p)
   base_log=log_prob(base_p)
   phi=make_basis(n, seed)
   M0=max(conv_sq_fft(base_p)); init_score=2n*M0
   best=base_p.copy(); best_score=init_score
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed+1)
   t0=time.monotonic(); end=t0+time_budget
   # number of restarts maybe 3-6 depending budget
   n_restarts = 3 if time_budget>600 else 2
   sub_budget = (end-t0)/n_restarts
   for r in range(n_restarts):
      if time.monotonic() >= end: break
      theta0 = 0.3*rng.standard_normal(phi.shape[1])
      # tau: maybe 0.1*M_best
      M_best = best_score/(2*n)
      tau = max(1e-12, 0.1*M_best)
      # estimate maxfev
      t_eval = max(0.03, 2e-5*n)  # rough
      maxfev = int(max(100, min(3000, (end - time.monotonic()) / max(time_budget? no sub_budget) / t_eval / 3.0)))? Need budget for this restart.
      rem = (end - time.monotonic()) / (n_restarts - r)
      maxfev = int(max(200, min(3000, rem / max(0.04, 3e-5*n))))
      res = minimize(param_soft_val_grad, theta0, args=(base_log, phi, tau), jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':maxfev//2, 'maxfev':maxfev, 'ftol':1e-10, 'gtol':1e-5, 'maxls':10})
      if res.x finite:
         p=softmax_prob(base_log + phi @ res.x)
         if p is not None:
            sc=score_norm(p)
            if sc<best_score: best=p; best_score=sc
   return best, best_score
```

```
def run_lbfgs_full(p, time_budget, seed=0):
   n=p.size
   best=p.copy(); best_score=score_norm(p)
   t0=time.monotonic(); end=t0+time_budget
   stages = [(0.10,0.30),(0.05,0.25),(0.02,0.20),(0.01,0.15),(0.005,0.10)] # tau_rel, frac
   for tau_rel, frac in stages:
      rem=end-time.monotonic()
      if rem<20: break
      budget=rem*frac
      M=best_score/(2*n)
      tau=max(1e-15, tau_rel*M)
      b0=log_prob(best)
      # estimate maxfev
      # measure one eval? Use rough
      t_eval=max(0.02, 2e-5*n)
      maxfev=int(max(200, min(5000, budget / t_eval / 3.0)))
      res=minimize(soft_val_grad_b, b0, args=(tau,), jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':max(50,maxfev//2), 'maxfev':maxfev, 'ftol':1e-11, 'gtol':1e-6, 'maxls':10})
      if res.x finite:
         pnew=softmax_prob(res.x)
         if pnew is not None:
            sc=score_norm(pnew)
            if sc<best_score: best=pnew; best_score=sc
   return best,best_score
```

def pool_best(pool):
    bs=float('inf'); bp=None
    for item in pool:
        if item[0] < bs:
            bs=item[0]; bp=item[1]
    return bs,bp

def add_pool(pool, p, cap=10):
    pn=normalize_nonneg(p)
    if pn is None: return pool
    sc=score_norm(pn)
    if not np.isfinite(sc): return pool
    bs,bp=pool_best(pool)
    if sc < bs or len(pool) == 0:
        pool.append((sc,pn))
    elif len(pool) < cap:
        pool.append((sc,pn))
    else:
        # replace worst if better
        worst_idx=max(range(len(pool)), key=lambda?) no lambda. find manually:
        worst_idx=0; worst_val=pool[0][0]
        for i in range(1,len(pool)):
            if pool[i][0] > worst_val: worst_val=pool[i][0]; worst_idx=i
        if sc < worst_val:
            pool[worst_idx]=(sc,pn)
    return pool
```

`modulation_sweep`:
```
def modulation_sweep(base, max_evals=120):
   n=base.size
   best=base.copy(); best_score=score_norm(base)
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   mods=[]
   for f in [1,2,3,4,6,8,12,16,24,32]:
      mods.append(np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x))
      mods.append(np.sin(2*np.pi*f*x))
   for q in [1,2,3,5,8,13]:
      mods.append(np.cos(np.pi*q*x*x))
      mods.append(np.sin(np.pi*q*x*x))
   eps_list=[0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.7]
   count=0
   for m in mods:
      if count >= max_evals: break
      for eps in eps_list:
         for sign in (1.0,-1.0):
            p=base*(1.0+sign*eps*m)
            p=np.maximum(p,0)
            s=p.sum()
            if s>0:
               p=p/s
               sc=score_norm(p)
               if sc<best_score:
                  best=p.copy(); best_score=sc
            count+=1
            if count>=max_evals: break
   return best, best_score
```
Could include random noise mods. Good.
```

Now, let's scrutinize gradient and objective in L-BFGS for full: We return `val = n*(M + tau log s)`. This val is not exactly n*J? `J=tau*logsumexp(c/tau)= M + tau log s`. Yes. Score=2n M. At tau small, val≈nM. Gradient `n*p*(gp-gm)` corresponds derivative of nJ. Good.

Parameter objective same. Good.

One subtle point: In `soft_val_grad_b`, `z=(c-M)/tau`. For c values much less than M, z very negative, e underflows; s ~ count of max. If only one max, log s=0, derivative weights one. Good. If tau too small (e.g., 1e-300), division may overflow? We set tau=max(tau,1e-300). c scale ~1e-4, M/tau ~1e296, z for c=0 is -1e296, exp underflow 0; no overflow because z negative. But computing `(c-M)/tau` where c-M negative finite / tiny => -inf maybe, okay. To be safe, if tau < 1e-12*M, use active weights. In `weights_from_c`, but in soft_val we can just set a minimum tau = 1e-6*M? For L-BFGS smoothness, not too small. In run_lbfgs tau stages 0.1M to 0.005M. Minimum 0.005M, not tiny. Good.

In `run_lbfgs_full`, tau_rel* M where M=best_score/(2n). For score 1.5, M=0.75/n. 0.005M=0.00375/n ~4.5e-7 for n=8192, okay.

Now, `projected_opt` details. Implement similar to mirror but projection. Use gradient of soft J (not exact). W with tau. `gp=grad_from_weights(w,p)`. Since val=nJ, step scaling maybe `g = gp / max(gp.max(), tiny)`. `norm_p = project_simplex(p - alpha*g_norm)`. alpha target 0.05*lr. Line search.
```
def projected_opt(p, time_budget, seed=0):
  n=p.size; best...; lr=1; fail=0
  while time:
    c,M; cur=2nM
    tau=0.05*M? anneal
    w=weights; gp=grad_from_weights(w,p)
    gmax=np.abs(gp).max(); if 0 break
    g=gp/gmax
    alpha=0.05*lr
    accepted=False
    for attempt in range(4):
       p_new=project_simplex(p - alpha*g)
       sc_new=score_norm(p_new)
       if sc_new < cur*(1-1e-12) or sc_new < best_score*(1-1e-12): accept
       alpha*=0.5
    if not: lr=max(lr*0.6,0.05); fail; reset if fail>40 with noise
```

`normalize_nonneg`:
```
def normalize_nonneg(x):
    try:
        a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a[np.isnan(a)] = 0.0  # cannot assign if scalar? a is array
    a[np.isinf(a)] = 0.0
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return a / s
```
But `a[np.isnan(a)]` works for 1D. If a read-only? np.asarray returns writable maybe. To be safe:
```
a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
```

`score_norm`:
```
def score_norm(a):
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return inf
    c=conv_sq_fft(a)
    m=float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0: m=0.0
    return float(2.0*n*m)
```
Assumes normalized. If not normalized, score wrong; ensure all calls after normalize.

`conv_sq_fft` for a maybe not contiguous; okay. Clip small negative? For score max, if all negative due underflow? not. But c may have tiny negative and max could be negative if p all zero? p sum1 so max positive. We can `if c.size: m=max(0.0, float(c.max()))`.

`grad_from_weights` returns finite? FFT can produce tiny negative. No issue. Could `d = np.ascontiguousarray(d)`.

`weights_from_c`:
```
def weights_from_c(c, tau):
    L=c.size
    M=float(c.max())
    w=np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    if M <= 0 or not np.isfinite(M):
        w[0]=1.0; return w
    if not np.isfinite(tau) or tau <= 1e-14 * max(1.0, abs(M)):
        mask = c >= M - 1e-12*max(1.0,abs(M))
        if not np.any(mask):
            idx=int(np.argmax(c)); w[idx]=1.0
        else:
            w[mask]=1.0
        s=w.sum()
        if s>0: w/=s
        else: w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0
        return w
    z=(c-M)/tau
    e=np.exp(z)
    s=float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0
    else:
        w=e/s
    return w
```
Use relative tau small threshold. But in mirror tau ~0.04M, not below. In L-BFGS tau min 0.005M, not. Good.

`project_simplex`:
```
def project_simplex(v, S=1.0):
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    v=np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    n=v.size
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css=np.cumsum(u)
    idx=np.arange(1, n+1, dtype=np.float64)
    tau_candidates=(css - S)/idx
    cond = u > tau_candidates
    if not np.any(cond):
        tau = (float(v.sum()) - S)/n
        res = np.maximum(v - tau, 0.0)
    else:
        # find last True
        rho = int(np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1]) + 1
        tau = (css[rho-1] - S)/rho
        res = np.maximum(v - tau, 0.0)
    # correct sum
    res_sum = float(res.sum())
    if res_sum <= 0 or not np.isfinite(res_sum):
        return np.full(n, S/n, dtype=np.float64)
    # Adjust the largest element to fix numerical sum
    res = res + (S - res_sum)/n
    # ensure nonnegative? If adding to all may make small negative if S<res_sum? But res_sum close. Better adjust max index.
    diff=S-res.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       idxmax=int(np.argmax(res))
       res[idxmax] += diff
       if res[idxmax] < 0:
          res = np.maximum(res,0.0); res/=max(res.sum(),1e-300)* (1.0/S?) hmm.
    return np.maximum(res,0.0)
```
Projection formula should give sum S within rounding; adding diff to max robust. But if diff negative and res[idxmax]+diff <0, not happen if diff small. Use:
```
res = np.maximum(res,0.0)
res -= res.sum()/n? no.
```
Standard after tau, sum exactly S mathematically. Rounding small. We can do:
```
diff = S - res.sum()
if diff != 0:
   idx = int(np.argmax(res))
   res[idx] += diff
   res = np.maximum(res,0.0)
   # if max became negative (shouldn't), renormalize
   rs=res.sum()
   if rs <=0 or not finite: return full
   if abs(rs-S)>S*1e-6: res = res*(S/rs)
```
Good.

`softmax_prob`:
```
def softmax_prob(x):
    x=np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size==0: return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
       x=np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = x - np.max(x)
    p=np.exp(x)
    s=float(p.sum())
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(x.size,1.0/x.size)
    p /= s
    return p
```

`run_param_opt_phi`:
```
def run_param_opt_phi(base_p, phi, time_budget, seed=0):
    pn=normalize_nonneg(base_p)
    if pn is None or pn.size<50 or time_budget<30:
       return pn if pn is not None else np.ones(300)/300, score...
    n=pn.size
    best=pn.copy(); best_score=score_norm(best)
    if phi is None: phi=make_basis(n, seed)
    M_cols=phi.shape[1]
    if M_cols==0: return best,best_score
    base_log=log_prob(best)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed+101)
    t0=time.monotonic(); end=t0+time_budget
    n_restarts = 2 if time_budget<200 else 3
    for r in range(n_restarts):
       rem=end-time.monotonic()
       if rem<30: break
       theta0=0.3*rng.standard_normal(M_cols)
       M_best=best_score/(2.0*n)
       tau=max(1e-15, 0.1*M_best)
       # estimate maxfev using one eval
       t1=time.monotonic(); soft_val_grad_b(log_prob(best), tau); t_eval=max(0.02, time.monotonic()-t1)
       sub_budget=(end-time.monotonic())/max(1,n_restarts-r)
       maxfev=int(max(100, min(3000, sub_budget / (t_eval*2.0))))
       try:
          res=minimize(param_soft_val_grad, theta0, args=(base_log, phi, tau), jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':max(50,maxfev//2), 'maxfev':maxfev, 'ftol':1e-10, 'gtol':1e-5, 'maxls':10})
          if np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
             pnew=softmax_prob(base_log + phi @ res.x)
             if pnew is not None:
                sc=score_norm(pnew)
                if sc<best_score:
                   best=pnew.copy(); best_score=sc
       except Exception:
          pass
    return best,best_score
```
The test eval `soft_val_grad_b(log_prob(best), tau)` is just for timing; okay. It may be same n but not param. Good.

`run_lbfgs_full` similar but full b. For timing, use `soft_val_grad_b(b0, tau)`.

```
def run_lbfgs_full(p, time_budget, seed=0):
    pn=normalize_nonneg(p)
    if pn is None or time_budget<30: return pn, score
    n=pn.size; best=pn; best_score=score_norm(best)
    t0=...; end=...
    stages=[(0.10,0.30),(0.05,0.25),(0.02,0.20),(0.01,0.15),(0.005,0.10)]
    for idx,(tau_rel, frac) in enumerate(stages):
        rem=end-time.monotonic(); if rem<20 break
        budget=rem*frac
        M=best_score/(2*n)
        tau=max(1e-15, tau_rel*M)
        b0=log_prob(best)
        t1=time.monotonic(); soft_val_grad_b(b0,tau); t_eval=max(0.02, time.monotonic()-t1)
        maxfev=int(max(200, min(5000, budget/(t_eval*2.0))))
        try: res=minimize(... options maxfev, maxiter max(50,maxfev//2))
        ...
    return best,best_score
```

`mirror_opt`: Need pass rescue optional. Ensure if time_budget <=0 return. Use `normalize_nonneg` first.
In reset, if rescue is None, create inv_sqrt(n). But calling init_inv_sqrt inside mirror for rescue each time? We can pass rescue. In propose, pass inv.
```
def mirror_opt(p, time_budget, seed=0, rescue=None):
   pn=normalize_nonneg(p)
   if pn is None: pn=np.ones(10);...
   if time_budget <=1: return pn, score_norm(pn)
   n=pn.size; best=pn.copy(); best_score=score_norm(pn)
   if rescue is None: rescue=init_inv_sqrt(n)
   else: rescue=normalize_nonneg(rescue)
   if rescue is None: rescue=best
   rng=default_rng(seed)
   t0=...; end=...
   lr=1.0; fail=0; it=0
   while time.monotonic() < end:
      c=conv_sq_fft(pn); M=...; cur=...
      if cur < best_score - 1e-12: best...
      if M<=1e-300: break
      progress=min(1.0, (time.monotonic()-t0)/max(time_budget,1e-6))
      tau=(0.10*(1.0-0.6*progress))*M
      if tau < 1e-12*max(1.0,abs(M)) or tau <=0: tau=1e-8*max(1.0,abs(M))
      w=weights_from_c(c,tau)
      g=grad_from_weights(w,pn)
      g -= float(np.dot(pn,g))
      gmax=float(np.abs(g).max())
      if gmax < 1e-16: break
      step=0.25*lr/gmax
      accepted=False
      for _ in range(4):
          expo=np.clip(-step*g, -30.0, 30.0)
          mult=np.exp(expo)
          pnew=pn*mult
          s=float(pnew.sum())
          if s <=0 or not finite: step*=0.5; continue
          pnew/=s
          scn=score_norm(pnew)
          if (scn < cur*(1.0-1e-12)) or (scn < best_score*(1.0-1e-12)):
             pn=pnew; accepted=True
             if scn < best_score: best=pnew.copy(); best_score=scn
             lr=min(lr*1.2, 20.0)
             break
          step*=0.5
      if not accepted:
          lr=max(lr*0.6, 0.05)
          fail += 1
          if fail > 30 or time.monotonic() < t0 + 5:
             # reset
             mix=0.1 + 0.2*rng.random()
             pn=(1.0-mix)*best + mix*rescue
             pn=np.maximum(pn,0.0)
             noise=np.exp(0.1*rng.standard_normal(n))
             pn=pn*noise
             pn=normalize_nonneg(pn)
             if pn is None: pn=best.copy()
             lr=1.0; fail=0
      it +=1
   return best,best_score
```
Reset condition `time < t0+5` not needed. Use fail>30.

`init_inv_sqrt`:
```
def init_inv_sqrt(n):
   if n<=0: return np.array([1.0])
   i=np.arange(1, n+1, dtype=np.float64)
   p=1.0/np.sqrt(i)
   p += 0.0
   p/=p.sum()
   return p
```
Add tiny end? no.

`init_chirp`:
```
def init_chirp(n, seed=0):
   if n<=1: return np.array([1.0])
   x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
   a=1.0 + 0.9*np.cos(np.pi*x*x)
   if seed:
      rng=default_rng(seed)
      a += 0.05*rng.standard_normal(n)
   a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<=0: return init_inv_sqrt(n)
   return a/s
```

`resize_sequence`:
```
def resize_sequence(x, n):
    a=normalize_nonneg(x)
    if a is None or n<=0: return None
    m=a.size
    if m==n: return a
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    b=np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return normalize_nonneg(np.maximum(b,0.0))
```

`modulation_sweep` as above. Ensure `score_norm` on normalized p.

`pool_best` and `add_pool` top-level.
```
def pool_best(pool):
    bs=float('inf'); bp=None
    for sc,p in pool:
       if sc < bs:
          bs=sc; bp=p
    return bs,bp

def add_pool(pool, p, cap=12):
    pn=normalize_nonneg(p)
    if pn is None: return pool
    sc=score_norm(pn)
    if not np.isfinite(sc): return pool
    bs,bp=pool_best(pool)
    if len(pool)==0 or sc < bs:
       pool.append((sc,pn))
    elif len(pool) < cap:
       pool.append((sc,pn))
    else:
       worst_idx=0; worst_val=pool[0][0]
       for i in range(1,len(pool)):
          if pool[i][0] > worst_val:
             worst_val=pool[i][0]; worst_idx=i
       if sc < worst_val:
          pool[worst_idx]=(sc,pn)
    return pool
```
This keeps best in pool but not sorted. For top selection, implement `get_top(pool, k)`.

```
       for i in range(1,len(rem)):
          if rem[i][0] < val:
             val=rem[i][0]; idx=i
       top.append(rem.pop(idx))
```

```python
"""
Search for low self-convolution peak non-negative sequences.

The score is scale invariant, so all optimization is done on probability
vectors p (sum 1) minimizing 2*n*max(p*p).  The code combines:
  - inverse-sqrt / chirp / provided starts,
  - low-dimensional Fourier/chirp parameterized L-BFGS starts,
  - full softmax-parameter L-BFGS on a smoothed max-concentration objective,
  - mirror-descent and projected-gradient polishing using exact FFT gradients.
"""

import time
import warnings

import numpy as np
from scipy.fft import fft, ifft
from scipy.optimize import minimize

warnings.filterwarnings("ignore")


def _next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_nonneg(x):
    try:
        a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return a / s


def conv_sq_fft(a):
    n = int(a.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = _next_pow2(L)
    fa = fft(a, N)
    c = ifft(fa * fa).real[:L]
    return c


def score_norm(a):
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    c = conv_sq_fft(a)
    m = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * m)


def grad_from_weights(w, p):
    """
    Gradient of sum_k w_k (p*p)_k with respect to p.
    For c_k = sum_i p_i p_{k-i}, dc_k/dp_j = 2 p_{k-j} when valid.
    """
    n = int(p.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    conv_len = n + L - 1
    N = _next_pow2(conv_len)
    fp = fft(p, N)
    fw = fft(w[::-1], N)
    conv = ifft(fp * fw).real
    d = conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
    return 2.0 * d


def weights_from_c(c, tau):
    L = int(c.size)
    w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    M = float(c.max()) if L else 0.0
    if M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(M):
        w[0] = 1.0
        return w

    scale = max(1.0, abs(M))
    if not np.isfinite(tau) or tau <= 1e-14 * scale:
        mask = c >= (M - 1e-12 * scale)
        if not np.any(mask):
            idx = int(np.argmax(c))
            w[idx] = 1.0
        else:
            w[mask] = 1.0
        s = float(w.sum())
        if s > 0.0:
            w /= s
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(c))
            w[idx] = 1.0
        return w

    z = (c - M) / tau
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        idx = int(np.argmax(c))
        w[idx] = 1.0
    else:
        w = e / s
    return w


def project_simplex(v, S=1.0):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v

    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css = np.cumsum(u)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    tau_cand = (css - S) / idx
    cond = u > tau_cand

    if not np.any(cond):
        tau = (float(v.sum()) - S) / n
        res = np.maximum(v - tau, 0.0)
    else:
        rho = int(np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1]) + 1
        tau = (css[rho - 1] - S) / rho
        res = np.maximum(v - tau, 0.0)

    rs = float(res.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(rs) or rs <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n, S / n, dtype=np.float64)

    diff = S - rs
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14 * max(1.0, S):
        im = int(np.argmax(res))
        res[im] += diff
        res = np.maximum(res, 0.0)
        rs2 = float(res.sum())
        if rs2 <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(rs2):
            return np.full(n, S / n, dtype=np.float64)
        if abs(rs2 - S) > 1e-8 * max(1.0, S):
            res *= (S / rs2)

    return np.maximum(res, 0.0)


def softmax_prob(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size == 0:
        return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = x - float(np.max(x))
    p = np.exp(x)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        p = np.full(x.size, 1.0 / x.size, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        p /= s
    return p


def log_prob(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
    b = np.log(p)
    b -= float(np.mean(b))
    return b


def soft_val_grad_b(b, tau):
    n = int(np.asarray(b).size)
    zgrad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if n < 2:
        return float("inf"), zgrad
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(b)):
        b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)

    p = softmax_prob(b)
    if p is None:
        return float("inf"), zgrad

    c = conv_sq_fft(p)
    M = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M < 0.0:
        M = 0.0

    tau = float(tau)
    if not np.isfinite(tau) or tau <= 0.0:
        tau = 1e-6 * max(1.0, abs(M))

    z = (c - M) / tau
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        w = e / s

    val = float(n * (M + tau * np.log(float(e.sum()))))
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return float("inf"), zgrad

    gp = grad_from_weights(w, p)
    gm = float(np.dot(p, gp))
    grad = n * p * (gp - gm)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return val, grad


def init_inv_sqrt(n):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    i = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    p = 1.0 / np.sqrt(i)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return p / s


def init_chirp(n, seed=0):
    if n <= 1:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = 1.0 + 0.9 * np.cos(np.pi * x * x)
    if seed:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        a += 0.05 * rng.standard_normal(n)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return init_inv_sqrt(n)
    return a / s


def resize_sequence(x, n):
    a = normalize_nonneg(x)
    if a is None or n <= 0:
        return None
    m = a.size
    if m == n:
        return a
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    return normalize_nonneg(b)


def mix_sequences(a, b, alpha):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    b = normalize_nonneg(b)
    if a is None and b is None:
        return None
    if a is None:
        return b
    if b is None:
        return a
    if a.size != b.size:
        b = resize_sequence(b, a.size)
        if b is None:
            return a
    p = (1.0 - float(alpha)) * a + float(alpha) * b
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    return normalize_nonneg(p)


def modulation_sweep(base, max_evals=160):
    pn = normalize_nonneg(base)
    if pn is None:
        return None, float("inf")

    n = pn.size
    best = pn.copy()
    bs = score_norm(best)

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    mods = []

    for f in [1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 8, 12, 16, 24, 32]:
        mods.append(np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x))
        mods.append(np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * x))

    for q in [1, 2, 3, 5, 8, 13, 21]:
        mods.append(np.cos(np.pi * q * x * x))
        mods.append(np.sin(np.pi * q * x * x))

    eps_list = [0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.5, 0.7]
    count = 0

    for m in mods:
        if count >= max_evals:
            break
        for eps in eps_list:
            for sign in (1.0, -1.0):
                p = base * (1.0 + sign * eps * m)
                p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
                s = float(p.sum())
                if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
                    p = p / s
                    sc = score_norm(p)
                    if sc < bs:
                        bs = sc
                        best = p.copy()
                count += 1
                if count >= max_evals:
                    break

    return best, bs


def make_basis(n, seed=0):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    cols = []

    max_nf = min(20, max(4, n // 16))
    for q in range(1, max_nf + 1):
        cols.append(np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(q) * x))
        cols.append(np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(q) * x))

    max_ch = min(10, max(2, n // 128))
    for q in range(1, max_ch + 1):
        cols.append(np.cos(np.pi * float(q) * x * x))
        cols.append(np.sin(np.pi * float(q) * x * x))

    target_extra = min(40, max(8, 100 - len(cols)))
    hi = max(2, min(n // 4, 800))
    for _ in range(target_extra):
        q = int(rng.integers(1, hi + 1))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        cols.append(np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(q) * x + ph))
        cols.append(np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(q) * x + ph))

    if not cols:
        phi = np.zeros((n, 1), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        phi = np.column_stack(cols)

    for j in range(phi.shape[1]):
        col = phi[:, j]
        mean = float(np.mean(col))
        std = float(np.std(col))
        if std < 1e-14:
            phi[:, j] = 0.0
        else:
            phi[:, j] = (col - mean) / std

    return phi


def param_soft_val_grad(theta, base_log, phi, tau):
    n = int(base_log.size)
    ncol = int(phi.shape[1])
    zgrad = np.zeros(ncol, dtype=np.float64)

    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if theta.size != ncol:
        return float("inf"), zgrad
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(theta)):
        theta = np.where(np.isfinite(theta), theta, 0.0)

    if ncol:
        lin = base_log + phi @ theta
    else:
        lin = base_log

    p = softmax_prob(lin)
    if p is None:
        return float("inf"), zgrad

    c = conv_sq_fft(p)
    M = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M < 0.0:
        M = 0.0

    tau = float(tau)
    if not np.isfinite(tau) or tau <= 0.0:
        tau = 1e-6 * max(1.0, abs(M))

    z = (c - M) / tau
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        w = e / s

    val = float(n * (M + tau * np.log(s)))
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return float("inf"), zgrad

    gp = n * grad_from_weights(w, p)
    if not ncol:
        return val, zgrad

    svec = p * gp
    ssum = float(np.sum(svec))
    mean_phi = phi.T @ p
    grad = phi.T @ svec - mean_phi * ssum

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad = np.zeros(ncol, dtype=np.float64)
    return val, grad


def run_param_opt_phi(base_p, phi, time_budget, seed=0):
    pn = normalize_nonneg(base_p)
    if pn is None:
        pn = init_inv_sqrt(3000)
    if pn.size < 50 or time_budget < 30.0:
        return pn, score_norm(pn)

    n = pn.size
    best = pn.copy()
    best_score = score_norm(best)

    if phi is None:
        phi = make_basis(n, seed)

    ncol = int(phi.shape[1])
    if ncol == 0:
        return best, best_score

    base_log = log_prob(best)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed + 101)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    end = t0 + float(time_budget)
    n_restarts = 3 if time_budget > 350.0 else 2

    for r in range(n_restarts):
        rem = end - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 30.0:
            break

        theta0 = 0.3 * rng.standard_normal(ncol)
        M_best = best_score / (2.0 * n)
        tau = max(1e-15, 0.1 * M_best)

        # Time one full-gradient evaluation to cap maxfev safely.
        btest = log_prob(best)
        t1 = time.monotonic()
        try:
            soft_val_grad_b(btest, tau)
            t_eval = max(0.02, time.monotonic() - t1)
        except Exception:
            t_eval = max(0.05, 2e-5 * n)

        sub_budget = (end - time.monotonic()) / max(1, n_restarts - r)
        maxfev = int(max(120, min(3000, sub_budget / max(0.05, 4.0 * t_eval))))

        try:
            res = minimize(
                param_soft_val_grad,
                theta0,
                args=(base_log, phi, tau),
                jac=True,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options={
                    "maxiter": max(50, maxfev // 2),
                    "maxfev": maxfev,
                    "ftol": 1e-10,
                    "gtol": 1e-5,
                    "maxls": 10,
                },
            )
            if np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
                pnew = softmax_prob(base_log + phi @ res.x)
                if pnew is not None:
                    sc = score_norm(pnew)
                    if sc < best_score:
                        best = pnew.copy()
                        best_score = sc
        except Exception:
            pass

    return best, best_score


def run_lbfgs_full(p, time_budget, seed=0):
    pn = normalize_nonneg(p)
    if pn is None:
        pn = init_inv_sqrt(3000)
    if pn.size < 50 or time_budget < 30.0:
        return pn, score_norm(pn)

    n = pn.size
    best = pn.copy()
    best_score = score_norm(best)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    end = t0 + float(time_budget)

    stages = [
        (0.10, 0.30),
        (0.05, 0.25),
        (0.02, 0.20),
        (0.01, 0.15),
        (0.005, 0.10),
    ]

    for tau_rel, frac in stages:
        rem = end - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 25.0:
            break
        budget = rem * float(frac)
        M = best_score / (2.0 * n)
        tau = max(1e-15, float(tau_rel) * M)
        b0 = log_prob(best)

        t1 = time.monotonic()
        try:
            soft_val_grad_b(b0, tau)
            t_eval = max(0.02, time.monotonic() - t1)
        except Exception:
            t_eval = max(0.05, 2e-5 * n)

        maxfev = int(max(200, min(6000, budget / max(0.05, 4.0 * t_eval))))

        try:
            res = minimize(
                soft_val_grad_b,
                b0,
                args=(tau,),
                jac=True,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options={
                    "maxiter": max(50, maxfev // 2),
                    "maxfev": maxfev,
                    "ftol": 1e-11,
                    "gtol": 1e-6,
                    "maxls": 10,
                },
            )
            if np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
                pnew = softmax_prob(res.x)
                if pnew is not None:
                    sc = score_norm(pnew)
                    if sc < best_score:
                        best = pnew.copy()
                        best_score = sc
        except Exception:
            pass

    return best, best_score


def mirror_opt(p, time_budget, seed=0, rescue=None):
    pn = normalize_nonneg(p)
    if pn is None:
        pn = init_inv_sqrt(3000)
    if pn.size < 2 or time_budget < 5.0:
        return pn, score_norm(pn)

    n = pn.size
    best = pn.copy()
    best_score = score_norm(best)

    if rescue is not None:
        rescue = normalize_nonneg(rescue)
    if rescue is None:
        rescue = init_inv_sqrt(n)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    end = t0 + float(time_budget)

    lr = 1.0
    fail = 0

    while time.monotonic() < end:
        c = conv_sq_fft(pn)
        M = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 1e-300:
            break
        cur = 2.0 * n * M
        if cur < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = cur
            best = pn.copy()

        elapsed = max(1e-6, time.monotonic() - t0)
        prog = min(1.0, elapsed / max(1e-6, time_budget))
        tau = (0.10 * (1.0 - 0.60 * prog)) * M
        if tau <= 1e-12 * max(1.0, abs(M)):
            tau = 1e-8 * max(1.0, abs(M))

        w = weights_from_c(c, tau)
        g = grad_from_weights(w, pn)
        g -= float(np.dot(pn, g))

        gmax = float(np.abs(g).max())
        if not np.isfinite(gmax) or gmax < 1e-16:
            break

        step = 0.25 * lr / gmax
        accepted = False

        for _ in range(4):
            expo = np.clip(-step * g, -30.0, 30.0)
            mult = np.exp(expo)
            pnew = pn * mult
            s = float(pnew.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
                step *= 0.5
                continue
            pnew /= s
            scn = score_norm(pnew)
            if (scn < cur * (1.0 - 1e-12)) or (scn < best_score * (1.0 - 1e-12)):
                pn = pnew
                accepted = True
                if scn < best_score:
                    best_score = scn
                    best = pnew.copy()
                lr = min(lr * 1.2, 20.0)
                break
            step *= 0.5

        if not accepted:
            lr = max(lr * 0.6, 0.05)
            fail += 1
            if fail > 30:
                mixalpha = float(rng.uniform(0.08, 0.28))
                pnew = (1.0 - mixalpha) * best + mixalpha * rescue
                pnew = np.maximum(pnew, 0.0)
                pnew *= np.exp(0.1 * rng.standard_normal(n))
                pnew = normalize_nonneg(pnew)
                if pnew is None:
                    pnew = best.copy()
                pn = pnew
                lr = 1.0
                fail = 0

    return best, best_score


def projected_opt(p, time_budget, seed=0):
    pn = normalize_nonneg(p)
    if pn is None:
        pn = init_inv_sqrt(3000)
    if pn.size < 2 or time_budget < 5.0:
        return pn, score_norm(pn)

    n = pn.size
    best = pn.copy()
    best_score = score_norm(best)
    rescue = init_inv_sqrt(n)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    end = t0 + float(time_budget)

    lr = 1.0
    fail = 0

    while time.monotonic() < end:
        c = conv_sq_fft(pn)
        M = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 1e-300:
            break
        cur = 2.0 * n * M
        if cur < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = cur
            best = pn.copy()

        elapsed = max(1e-6, time.monotonic() - t0)
        prog = min(1.0, elapsed / max(1e-6, time_budget))
        tau = (0.08 * (1.0 - 0.50 * prog)) * M
        if tau <= 1e-12 * max(1.0, abs(M)):
            tau = 1e-8 * max(1.0, abs(M))

        w = weights_from_c(c, tau)
        gp = grad_from_weights(w, pn)
        gmax = float(np.abs(gp).max())
        if not np.isfinite(gmax) or gmax < 1e-16:
            break
        g = gp / gmax

        alpha = 0.08 * lr
        accepted = False

        for _ in range(4):
            pnew = project_simplex(pn - alpha * g, 1.0)
            scn = score_norm(pnew)
            if (scn < cur * (1.0 - 1e-12)) or (scn < best_score * (1.0 - 1e-12)):
                pn = pnew
                accepted = True
                if scn < best_score:
                    best_score = scn
                    best = pnew.copy()
                lr = min(lr * 1.15, 20.0)
                break
            alpha *= 0.5

        if not accepted:
            lr = max(lr * 0.6, 0.05)
            fail += 1
            if fail > 35:
                mixalpha = float(rng.uniform(0.08, 0.25))
                pnew = (1.0 - mixalpha) * best + mixalpha * rescue
                pnew = np.maximum(pnew, 0.0)
                pnew *= np.exp(0.08 * rng.standard_normal(n))
                pnew = normalize_nonneg(pnew)
                if pnew is None:
                    pnew = best.copy()
                pn = pnew
                lr = 1.0
                fail = 0

    return best, best_score


def pool_best(pool):
    bs = float("inf")
    bp = None
    for sc, p in pool:
        if sc < bs:
            bs = sc
            bp = p
    return bs, bp


def add_pool(pool, p, cap=12):
    pn = normalize_nonneg(p)
    if pn is None:
        return pool
    sc = score_norm(pn)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return pool

    if len(pool) == 0 or sc < pool_best(pool)[0]:
        pool.append((sc, pn))
    elif len(pool) < cap:
        pool.append((sc, pn))
    else:
        worst_idx = 0
        worst_val = pool[0][0]
        for i in range(1, len(pool)):
            if pool[i][0] > worst_val:
                worst_val = pool[i][0]
                worst_idx = i
        if sc < worst_val:
            pool[worst_idx] = (sc, pn)

    return pool


def get_top(pool, k):
    top = []
    rem = list(pool)
    while len(top) < k and rem:
        idx = 0
        val = rem[0][0]
        for i in range(1, len(rem)):
            if rem[i][0] < val:
                val = rem[i][0]
                idx = i
        top.append(rem.pop(idx))
    return top


def propose_candidate():
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t_start + 2360.0

    try:
        prov = normalize_nonneg(height_sequence_1)
    except Exception:
        prov = None

    if prov is not None and prov.size < 50:
        prov = None

    pool = []
    if prov is not None:
        add_pool(pool, prov)

    lengths = []
    if prov is not None and 1000 <= prov.size <= 12000:
        lengths.append(int(prov.size))
    if 8192 not in lengths:
        lengths.append(8192)

    ulengths = []
    for nv in lengths:
        if nv not in ulengths:
            ulengths.append(nv)
    if len(ulengths) > 2:
        keep = []
        for nv in ulengths:
            if nv == 8192 or len(keep) < 2:
                keep.append(nv)
        ulengths = keep[:2]

    # Initial diverse candidates.
    for n in ulengths:
        inv = init_inv_sqrt(n)
        add_pool(pool, inv)
        chirp = init_chirp(n, 1)
        add_pool(pool, chirp)

        if prov is not None:
            rp = resize_sequence(prov, n)
            if rp is not None:
                add_pool(pool, rp)

        rng0 = np.random.default_rng(1234 + n)
        p1 = inv * (1.0 + 0.25 * rng0.standard_normal(n))
        add_pool(pool, p1)
        p2 = inv * (1.0 + 0.15 * rng0.standard_normal(n))
        add_pool(pool, p2)

        # Low-dimensional modulation sweep from the best current candidate of this length.
        base_this = inv
        bs_this = score_norm(base_this)
        for sc, cand in pool:
            if cand.size == n and sc < bs_this:
                bs_this = sc
                base_this = cand
        mb, _ = modulation_sweep(base_this, max_evals=160)
        if mb is not None:
            add_pool(pool, mb)

    # Quick local polishing of the current top candidates.
    quick_total = max(120.0, min(600.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.20))
    top = get_top(pool, 6)
    if top:
        per = quick_total / float(len(top))
        for i, (sc, p) in enumerate(top):
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic() - 40.0
            if rem <= 15.0:
                break
            budget = min(per * 1.25, max(20.0, rem / max(1.0, float(len(top) - i))))
            p2, sc2 = mirror_opt(p, budget, seed=1000 + i, rescue=init_inv_sqrt(p.size))
            add_pool(pool, p2)

    # Parameterized global search on the best length.
    best_score, best_seq = pool_best(pool)
    if best_seq is not None:
        param_total = max(120.0, min(650.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.20))
        if param_total > 50.0:
            n_p = best_seq.size
            phi_p = make_basis(n_p, 777)
            p2, sc2 = run_param_opt_phi(best_seq, phi_p, param_total * 0.65, seed=555)
            add_pool(pool, p2)

            if 8192 != n_p and (deadline - time.monotonic()) > 120.0:
                n2 = 8192
                base2 = init_inv_sqrt(n2)
                for sc, cand in pool:
                    if cand.size == n2 and sc < score_norm(base2):
                        base2 = cand
                phi2 = make_basis(n2, 778)
                bud2 = min(300.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.18)
                if bud2 > 50.0:
                    p3, sc3 = run_param_opt_phi(base2, phi2, bud2, seed=444)
                    add_pool(pool, p3)

    # Basin hopping via mixing good candidates.
    best_score, best_seq = pool_best(pool)
    if best_seq is not None:
        hop_total = max(80.0, min(350.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.12))
        if hop_total > 50.0:
            top5 = get_top(pool, 5)
            if top5:
                per = hop_total / min(4.0, float(len(top5)))
                for i in range(min(4, len(top5))):
                    rem = deadline - time.monotonic() - 40.0
                    if rem <= 20.0:
                        break
                    other = top5[(i + 1) % len(top5)][1]
                    pm = mix_sequences(best_seq, other, 0.35 + 0.08 * float(i))
                    if pm is not None and pm.size == best_seq.size:
                        p2, sc2 = mirror_opt(pm, min(per, rem), seed=4000 + i, rescue=best_seq)
                        add_pool(pool, p2)

    # Full-dimensional L-BFGS polishing of top candidates.
    lbfgs_total = max(150.0, min(800.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.25))
    top2 = get_top(pool, 2)
    if top2:
        per = lbfgs_total / float(len(top2))
        for i, (sc, p) in enumerate(top2):
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic() - 50.0
            if rem <= 60.0:
                break
            budget = min(per * 1.5, max(60.0, rem / max(1.0, float(len(top2) - i))))
            p2, sc2 = run_lbfgs_full(p, budget, seed=2000 + i)
            add_pool(pool, p2)

    # Final focused polishing.
    best_score, best_seq = pool_best(pool)
    if best_seq is not None:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic() - 35.0
        if rem > 80.0:
            n = best_seq.size
            b1 = rem * 0.35
            p2, sc2 = mirror_opt(best_seq, b1, seed=3000, rescue=init_inv_sqrt(n))
            add_pool(pool, p2)

        best_score, best_seq = pool_best(pool)
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic() - 35.0
        if rem > 60.0:
            b2 = rem * 0.30
            p2, sc2 = projected_opt(best_seq, b2, seed=3001)
            add_pool(pool, p2)

        best_score, best_seq = pool_best(pool)
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic() - 35.0
        if rem > 80.0:
            b3 = rem * 0.40
            p2, sc2 = run_lbfgs_full(best_seq, b3, seed=3002)
            add_pool(pool, p2)

    best_score, best_seq = pool_best(pool)
    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = init_inv_sqrt(3000)

    seq = normalize_nonneg(best_seq)
    if seq is None:
        seq = np.full(3000, 1.0 / 3000.0, dtype=np.float64)

    return [float(x) for x in seq]
```
